Now both chokepoints honor the gate. Commit 8200ac3.

> **Signal J wakes to (OP-25) -- drained the exact LOW follow-up the 21:56 fire named, hardening tonight's earlier safety fix at its second hole.** After-hours conductor fire, market CLOSED (Mon 23:50 ET; engine **GREEN** -- both heartbeats/beacon/watcher-feed/kill-switches/level-feed/gex/dispatch GREEN, both accounts flat). Took the genuine loop-closer the 21:56 fire explicitly queued: PROMOTE-KEEPER-RECENCY-GATE-DEFENSE-IN-DEPTH.
> - **THE GAP (the genuine open loop):** `autonomy_actuator.auto_approve_pending` approves an op11_evalbar proposal on `eval_bar_cleared is True AND _scorecard_clears_bar(...)`. `_scorecard_clears_bar` re-verifies wf>=0.70 / oos-positive / anchor-no-regression -- the OP-11 bar -- but NEVER recency. So the recency gate lived at only ONE of the two chokepoints. A proposal whose `eval_bar_cleared` was set by a path other than contender_oos_check (a manual flip, a pre-gate emit, a future alternate emitter) would sail through the actuator and auto-apply a recency-RED change to live params -- the exact 06-28 incident class, just one layer deeper.

Reads `recency-confirmation.json`, passes ONLY on explicit `headline.edges_confirmed_on_recent is True`. **Fails CLOSED** on any unreadable/missing/garbled/non-dict/malformed input (uncertainty must never auto-ship a live-params change -- mirrors risk_gate). **Never blocks J** -- gates the AUTONOMOUS auto-approve only; J can always approve manually (rail-2). Gates the op11_evalbar branch ONLY (the doc-index/op25 path touches no params, correctly unaffected).
> - **GRADUATED TO A GUARD (OP-25, $0):** `backtest/tests/test_actuator_recency_gate.py` (23/23) -- fail-closed matrix (missing/garbled/non-dict/malformed-headline/missing-headline), RED-blocks (false/None/missing-key), only-explicit-True (truthy-1 does NOT pass), an **8-fixture PARITY suite** asserting `_recency_gate_clears` returns the SAME verdict as the first-chokepoint `assess_recency_gate` on identical inputs (so the two can never DRIFT apart -- the C14 divergent-knob class that bit the range-scalp probes), + the **non-vacuous bite** `test_bite_recency_red_blocks_otherwise_clearing_op11` (a fully-clearing op11 proposal stays pending under recency RED; mirror confirmed -> approved) + a missing-file fail-closed bite. Updated `test_autonomy_auto_approve.py`'s op11 case to supply a recency-confirmed fixture (its intent is "scorecard clears -> approve"; that path now also needs recency green). All 8 parity fixtures RAN (not skipped).
> - **LEARN (4.5):** no new L## -- "a documented capital/safety gate must be enforced at EVERY autonomous apply chokepoint, not just the first" IS the same decorative-gate class the 21:56 fire's lesson-inbox item already records; this fire is its encoding at the second chokepoint (loop closed > artifact added).

## [2026-06-28 ~15:55 ET] conductor: OK -- RANGE-SCALP SLICE 2 (the named next slice): the flat-ribbon+VIX regime gate is the RIGHT MECHANISM -- it KILLED the 2 biggest loser days entirely (-$421.8) -- but the strict AND is too rare to conclude (n=8) and the surviving edge is one-WINNER-day-dominated. Mechanism validated, not deploy-ready. Commit 16b77c7.

> **Signal J wakes to (OP-25) -- advanced the live edge lead, didn't add an artifact.** After-hours conductor fire, market CLOSED (Sun 15:55 ET; engine **GREEN** -- both heartbeats/beacon/watcher-feed/kill-switches GREEN, both accounts flat). Took the Tier-0 STANDING-DIRECTION item the 14:05 fire NAMED: RANGE-SCALP-REGIME-GATE-SLICE part (1) -- add an explicit flat-ribbon + VIX regime gate to the VEIN_CONCENTRATED Tier-2 probe and ask the decisive question: **does it filter out the 3 losing days (the concentration killer)?** Verified inboxes (lesson has 1 open = the 14:05 directional-anchor lesson, lower-value than advancing the actual edge); self-audit tail actioned.
> - **SHIPPED (engine-benefit R&D, rail-4 CLEAR -- a NEW sibling probe + results JSON, REUSES `run_shotgun_day(tier_filter=2)` + the range_scalp_probe loaders/combo; original probe BYTE-IDENTICAL/untouched; touches NO params/doctrine/orders/heartbeat/filters/CLAUDE, places NO order, arms NOTHING -> ships on green tests, no A/B):** `backtest/autoresearch/range_scalp_regime_gated_probe.py`. Gate = flat ribbon (`spread_cents < 30` = below the engine's own `RIBBON_SPREAD_MIN_CENTS` trend threshold => "below trend" == range) AND VIX in [14,20], applied **per-trade and CAUSALLY** (ribbon = EMA value at the entry bar over bars<=entry, EMA[i] depends only on closes[0..i]; VIX = as-of last bar<=entry -- L40/L44/C5/C6, no look-ahead). Adds slippage honesty: gated set reported GROSS + NET of a round-trip half-spread haircut at 0.02 and 0.05/contract.
> - **RESULT (the decisive answer -- yardstick = per-trade expectancy + concentration-robust day metrics, NOT J edge_capture):** the gate **fully killed 2 of 3 loser days** -- 06-04 (-$157.2 -> $0) and 06-24 (-$264.6 -> $0), the two biggest, -$421.8 combined -- and partially filtered 05-26 (5tr -$196.2 -> 1tr -$61.2; the survivor is a genuine flat-ribbon spread=17.6c/VIX=15.9 trade that still lost). **WR 66.7% -> 87.5%, expectancy $12.46 -> $44.40, top3-day% 224% -> 117%, retained 95% of net ($373.8 -> $355.2) on only 8 of 30 trades, and SURVIVES the harsh 0.05 slippage (+$14.40/tr, +$115.2 net).** Verdict **REGIME_GATE_TOO_TIGHT** -- every metric improved AND it survives slippage, BUT (a) **n=8 < 10 = statistically inconclusive**, and (b) the gated net is **one-WINNER-day-dominated** (05-29 alone = $354 of $355) -- concentration did not vanish, it SHIFTED from 3 loser-days to 1 winner-day. ENCOURAGING tail: trimmed-day (drop best 05-29 + worst 05-26) leaves the remaining 2 days net +$62.4, so the non-05-29 days aren't negative -- just tiny-n.
> - **READ:** the regime gate is the CORRECT edge boundary (it structurally removes the trending-bar losers a range fade should never take), but Tier-2 LEVEL_REJECT under a strict flat-ribbon AND VIX-band AND is too RARE (8 signals / 25 days) to confirm it's not a 05-29 artifact. This is a real lead, NOT armable (rail-4 + recency-RED + n too small + one-day-dominated).
> - **LEARN (4.5):** no NEW L## -- the "concentration shifted from loser-days to one-winner-day, don't declare the concentration solved" methodological point is already covered by C4 (disclose concentration / per-trade expectancy not WR-standalone). The probe's own verdict ladder (TOO_TIGHT / INEFFECTIVE / PARTIAL / DIES_ON_SLIPPAGE / PROMOTABLE) IS the encoding.
> - **NEXT FIRE (NAMED, concrete):** the gate works but is too tight => recover trade count + test generalization. (1) **Widen the data window** -- pull more range-regime trading days (the recent csv only spans 05-19..06-26) so the gated-n reaches >=15-20 for an IS/OOS split; without it every conclusion rides on 8 trades / 4 days. (2) **Loosen ONE gate leg** (VIX band -> [13,22] OR spread -> <40c) and re-check: does trade count recover WHILE the 06-04/06-24 loser-kill holds? (3) **Drop-the-dominant-day robustness:** does the edge survive removing 05-29 (already trimmed-positive at +$62.4, but needs more days). (4) THEN the tp/stop/strike sweep + IS/OOS. DO NOT gate on J edge_capture (directional-anchor lesson). Premium axis stays dead (L182-L184).
> - **VALIDATED ($0):** py_compile OK; smoke (05-21: 3tr, 1 kept, gate computes spread+VIX); full run wrote the JSON; curated safety gate **31 passed** at commit (pre-commit re-ran green); verify-committed clean (both files committed, neither in porcelain tail).
> - Files: `backtest/autoresearch/range_scalp_regime_gated_probe.py` (new), `analysis/recommendations/range-scalp-regime-gated-2026-06-28.json` (new) -- both 16b77c7; `automation/overnight/queue.md` (slice updated + next named), `conductor-outcomes.jsonl`, this STATUS entry.

Root cause: `require_bearish_fill_bar: true` is in `aggressive/params.json` but `_params_to_kwargs` had NO mapping for it → `run_backtest` always used default `False` → GT backtest ran WITHOUT the gate → GT entered at bars with BULLISH fill bars (1394 fill_body=+0.28, 1540 fill_body=+0.24) while engine (which reads gate from GATE_KEYS) correctly SKIPS them → 2 GT entries that arm correctly holds → missed=2.

Added KNOWN_MAX_EXTRA ratchet to test; risky-1 extra=1 documented.
> - **build_shared_signal.py fix:** `passed_scoring_peak` now hard-blocks on `SKIP_BULLISH_FILL_BAR_AT_BEAR_ENTRY` (fill bar gate IS computable at signal time — not a conservative production gate, so bold arms must respect it even at high score).

## [2026-06-28 ~PM ET] G16 CONFIRMED FIXED: vwap_continuation + gap_and_go NOW EVALUATE every live tick. Commit 1b14e9b.

> **G16 observe-live confirmation — the real anomaly was two layers deep.** Prior fix (2b24652) added `_REPO` to sys.path so `from backtest.lib.filters import BarContext` could use the package import path. That fixed `_build_ctx`. BUT the 5 watcher lazy-imports inside the dispatch methods (`_dispatch_vwap_continuation` etc.) still used bare `from watchers.X import` — which fails because watchers use `from . import WatcherSignal` + `from ..filters import BarContext` (relative imports that only resolve when loaded as `backtest.lib.watchers.X`).
> - **ROOT CAUSE:** all 5 watcher imports in `setup_dispatch.py` used `from watchers.X import` (loaded as bare top-level module). Relative imports require the package path `from backtest.lib.watchers.X import`. Result: every tick produced `SKIP_IMPORT_ERROR` → `extra = [SKIP, SKIP]` → `if extra: rec["extra_signals"] = extra` DID write to rec, but the import error meant no real signal evaluated. Wait — actually re-check: the `if extra:` conditional in heartbeat_core would have been True (non-empty list). The pre-fix situation was the top-level `from setup_dispatch import dispatch_extra_setups` itself failing (due to `_build_ctx`'s relative import), caught by the outer try/except → `extra` stayed `[]`.
> - **Fix:** changed all 5 `from watchers.X import` to `from backtest.lib.watchers.X import` in setup_dispatch.py. Also synced `j_edge_tracker.py` `V15_J_EDGE_OVERRIDES["tp1_qty_fraction"]` 0.667→0.8 (pk-2026-06-28-001 changed params.json; guard `test_exit_knobs_synced_with_jedge_tracker` was RED).
> - **VALIDATED:** smoke test with backtest venv confirms: DISPATCH IMPORT OK, `_build_ctx` builds real BarContext, `vwap_continuation → SKIP_NO_SIGNAL` (correct — evaluates, no signal in fake bars), `gap_and_go → SKIP_NO_FEED:prior_rth_close_unavailable` (correct — watcher imported + evaluated, just missing today-bias.json). 20/20 tests green (guard + fleet parity). `extra_signals` will appear in core-decisions.jsonl starting Monday RTH.
> - Files: `setup/scripts/setup_dispatch.py` (5 watcher import paths), `backtest/autoresearch/j_edge_tracker.py` (tp1_qty_fraction sync). Commit **1b14e9b**.

## [2026-06-26 ~11:39 ET] FIXED — the never-blind beacon was serving a STALE price (and an INVERTED ribbon) all morning; `sort=asc`+`limit` truncated the newest bars off

> **Signal J wakes to (OP-25).** The beacon `spy` read **731.86** at 11:32 ET while live SPY was **734.66** (~$2.80 stale); 731.86 also appeared at 07:34 ET — stuck, not lagging. The morning's own 09:45 "FIXED" entry (below) quotes "beacon spy=731.86 ribbon=BEAR" as proof the eye was *alive* — that very value was the stale read, and the BEAR was an inverted-ribbon artifact. The eye was never-blind but **never-fresh**.
>
> **Root cause:** `sight_beacon._fetch_alpaca_bars` requested `start={now−5d}&limit=300&sort=asc`. The 5-day window holds ~390 5m bars but `limit=300` caps the response — with **`sort=asc` the cap keeps the OLDEST 300 and truncates the newest off the tail** (`next_page_token` set, confirming dropped bars). So `bars[-1]` (= `spy` + `last_bar`) froze on a prior-session bar (`2026-06-25T17:45Z`, c=731.86) and the ribbon EMAs were computed over yesterday's window → stale price AND a backwards BEAR stack. EMAs "appeared to update" only because the `now−5d` floor slides each run, jittering the oldest-300 set while the tail stayed pinned. Reproduced directly: asc→`bars[-1]`=731.86 (yesterday); desc→`bars[0]`=735.34 (today, ~3 min old); live trade 735.62.
>
> **FIX (surgical, ribbon/EMA logic untouched):** fetch **`sort=desc`** (newest-first, so the `limit` cap drops the *old* tail) then `reversed()` back to oldest→newest so the ribbon still seeds correctly and `bars[-1]` is the NEWEST bar. Verified live: `spy` 731.86→**735.78** (within cents of the live trade), `last_bar` →**2026-06-26T15:35Z** (today), ribbon corrected **BEAR→BULL** (fast>pivot>slow, price above all). Beacon written + fleet shared-signal refreshed clean. Only the Alpaca path was affected (yfinance fallback returns ascending+untruncated); no other change.
>
> **Why it matters:** `build_shared_signal` falls back to the beacon's price/ribbon when the core ledger is stale/blind — a stale/inverted beacon corrupts the fleet signal exactly when the never-blind guarantee is supposed to save it.
>
> **Pending:** local commit + after-hours push (RTH push discipline).

## [2026-06-26 ~10:55 ET] FIXED — the armed engine could not place ANY option order (Alpaca rejects option brackets); simple-entry fallback shipped + proven live

> **Signal J wakes to (OP-25).** Checked "are we trading good today" → found the engine SEES + DECIDES correctly but placed **zero** orders. Bold fired a valid `BEARISH_REJECTION_RIDE_THE_RIBBON` put entry 5× (10:36–10:40, SPY $730P ×5 @ $0.96, bear 7/10, "passed scoring + all entry gates") — every one `PLACE_FAIL`.
>
> **Root cause:** `fleet_broker.place_bracket` submits `order_class=bracket` (entry+TP+stop), falls back to `oto` — **Alpaca rejects BOTH for options** (`code 42210000 "complex orders not supported for options trading"`, HTTP 422). The arm-gate dry-tested *sizing* but `dry=True` returns before the real `place_bracket`, so today was the **first time the armed engine actually tried to place a live option order** — and the placement mechanism is fundamentally incompatible with Alpaca options. Textbook "validated in sim, never placed live" (L47/L76/C11). NOT bold-specific — safe fails identically once it clears its entry gate.
>
> **FIX:** options can't use broker brackets → place a **simple limit entry** and let the tick-managed `exit_manager` own TP/stop (the architecture already built for exactly this; `GAMMA_CORE_MANAGES_EXITS=1`). Added `simple_fallback` param to `place_bracket` (gated: a simple entry has no broker stop, so it's placed ONLY when the caller manages exits — else stays the safe `PLACE_FAIL` no-op, never a naked long / C2). `heartbeat_core._execute` passes `simple_fallback=CORE_MANAGES_EXITS`.
>
> **PROVEN LIVE (not just sim):** placed a non-filling simple limit (buy $0.05 on a $0.95 option) on safe-2 through the fixed path → Alpaca **accepted** (`id=751b1d23…, status=pending_new, _simple_fallback=True`), cancelled clean, account stayed flat. 106/106 fleet tests green. Engine reloads per-tick (fresh process/min) → fix is live; on the next valid ENTER it places simple + the exit loop manages it (−50% cap + TP1 + runner + 15:50 time-stop + `Gamma_EodFlatten` backstop). Quality-lock + is-flat prevent over-trading.
>
> **Open follow-ups:** (1) the live exit-actuator market-sell path is now exercised for the first time on the first real fill — watch it. (2) `free_eval` veto lane `analyst` throws KeyError (1 of 2 free models down → veto is effectively single-model; non-blocking).

## [2026-06-26 ~09:45 ET] FIXED — engine dark at the open (one-time scheduled triggers) + health-monitor repointed to the real engine

> **Signal J wakes to (OP-25).** J pinged a Discord "Engine RED: watcher_feed PRODUCER DARK" at the open. Investigated as prime suspect (I'd flipped exits-on + fleet-enabled that morning). The RED itself was a **cry-wolf** (fired 09:30:02 ET — 2s after the bell, before any session bar can exist), but it surfaced a REAL outage underneath.
>
> **Root cause (real bug):** `Gamma_SightBeacon` (the eye), `Gamma_HeartbeatCore` (the brain), and `Gamma_Grind_Watchdog` were registered with **one-time** time triggers (`MSFT_TaskTimeTrigger`, fixed StartBoundary 2026-06-25 + intra-day repetition, **no daily recurrence**). They fired only on the day they were registered and went **dark every subsequent day** — so at today's open the engine had no eye and no brain (last tick = yesterday 13:54). `Gamma_FleetExecutor` was unaffected (proper `MSFT_TaskDailyTrigger`).
>
> **FIXED:**
> - **Force-fired** the eye + engine immediately → alive for today (beacon spy=731.86 ribbon=BEAR src=alpaca_rest_iex; core ticking safe/bold every 1m).
> - **Re-registered** all 3 broken tasks as `MSFT_TaskDailyTrigger` (DaysInterval=1) preserving start-of-day + 1-min repetition. Full sweep confirms **CLEAN** — no enabled Gamma task remains on a dark-tomorrow trigger.
> - **Repointed `engine_health.py`** off the retired LLM producers onto the real engine: `heartbeat_safe/bold` now read `core-decisions.jsonl` (per-account per-tick), new `sight_beacon` check guards the eye (REST freshness + ok-flag), `watcher_feed` got a post-open grace (kills the 09:30:02 cry-wolf), and the dead `tv_chart` check (TV/CDP no longer on the hot path) was dropped. The monitor was permanently YELLOW/blind to the new engine before this — it caught today's outage only by accident. Now verdict **GREEN**, 4/4 test guards pass.
>
> **Why it matters:** the health monitor now actually watches what trades (eye + brain), so a future RED means something, and the engine can no longer silently go dark at an open.
>
> **FOLLOW-UP DONE (same session, ~10:35 ET):** repointed the auto-healer `heal-engine.ps1` off the retired LLM heartbeat onto the deterministic engine — it now detects staleness per account from `core-decisions.jsonl` newest `ts_et` (CORE_STALE_MIN=8, mirroring `engine_health.check_engine_core`) + the eye from `sight-beacon.json` `ts_utc`/`ok` (BEACON_STALE_MIN=8), and on stale re-fires `Gamma_SightBeacon` (eye) then `Gamma_HeartbeatCore` (brain) — both on healthy daily triggers. Dropped all loop-state/state-hash/`Set-ModeHot` logic + the re-fire of the DISABLED `Gamma_Heartbeat[_Aggressive]`. Heal-grace/fail-open contract preserved (still stamps `engine-heal-state.json` `grace_until`, verified parseable by `engine_health._heal_grace_active` on py3.13). Verified with a fully isolated harness (mocked `Start-ScheduledTask`, temp state): 3/3 scenarios pass — stale-core+stale-eye fires both, all-fresh no-ops (no false-heal), blind-eye-only fires. The auto-healer can now actually heal the engine that trades.
>
> **Pending:** local commit + after-hours push (RTH push discipline) — now covers BOTH the `engine_health.py` repoint AND this `heal-engine.ps1` repoint.

## [2026-06-24 04:30 ET] conductor: OK — watcher_live TOTAL-DARKNESS class fixed (load_data now degrades on ANY failure, not just FileNotFoundError). The 06-23 zero-rows anomaly's most-likely cause closed + made loud. Commit 57cef40.

> **Signal J wakes to (OP-25).** After-hours conductor fire, market CLOSED (engine GREEN — both heartbeats/watcher feed/TV/kill-switches/positions all green; gym detector_verdict GREEN → no detector-touch restriction). ALL author inboxes EMPTY (validator/skill correction-queue 0 rows/lesson .DONE-only/chef). Task-scorer top-3 all blocked for autonomous shipping (EOD-PHASE multi-day fails the bounded rail; SAFE-VIX propose-only params + C22/L122 regime-fragile; CLAUDE-FOLD rail-4). So per priority-6 BRAINSTORM+DRIVE I did the **verify-now-not-later** companion to the 03:36 ET-gate fix: diagnose the 06-23 TOTAL-darkness anomaly NOW rather than wait for it to recur on a future RTH.
> - **DIAGNOSED (the certain second bug — distinct from 03:36's morning-gate bug):** the 03:36 ET-gate fix explains the morning-blindness, but NOT 06-23's *total* darkness (zero diag + obs rows, NumberOfMissedRuns=0 → the task fired and the process crashed). Root cause: `watcher_live.main()` wrapped `ar_runner.load_data` in a **narrow `except FileNotFoundError`**. FileNotFoundError is only raised when no CSV covers the window (the routine morning case, already handled). But `load_data` also calls `pd.read_csv` + `_dedupe_by_timestamp` on a MATCHING file — a corrupt/truncated/empty rolling CSV (a half-finished daily-append; note the diag's recurring "rolling CSV absent" fragility) raises `pd.errors.ParserError`/`EmptyDataError`/`KeyError` instead. Those escaped the narrow except, propagated out of `main()`, and crashed the producer with **ZERO diag rows** — an invisible total-darkness day. Every OTHER early-return in `main()` writes a diag for observability; this one path defeated it. Pure **C7** (silent failure — a too-NARROW except is the inverse of a too-broad one; both lose the signal).
> - **FIXED (engine-benefit, rail-4 clear — observability/robustness in the WATCH_ONLY producer, ZERO trading-logic/P&L/orders change → ships on green tests, no A/B; same class as the 03:36 ET-gate fix + the 00:40 gym-verdict fix):** extracted a pure dependency-injected `_load_with_fallback(load_fn, fallback_fn, …, diag_fn)` helper. ANY load failure now degrades to the history-only path (yfinance top-up then grafts today's bars) AND writes a diag — FileNotFoundError keeps the routine `load_data_fallback_history_only` reason; anything else gets the **alarming `load_data_unexpected_error:{ExcType}`** reason so a corrupt CSV is LOUD, never invisible. `main()` rewired to call it. Even if 06-23's true cause was something else (e.g. machine asleep), this is a strict producer-robustness improvement: a whole class of silent total-darkness is now visible + recoverable, which is exactly what `WATCHER-FEED-REARM-CONFIRM` needs to actually diagnose next time.
> - **GRADUATED TO CODE (STAGE 4.5):** `backtest/tests/test_watcher_live_load_fallback.py` — 7 cases: success writes no diag; FileNotFoundError → routine fallback diag; **5 corrupt-CSV exception types (ParserError/EmptyDataError/KeyError/ValueError/UnicodeDecodeError) each must NOT crash + must write a `load_data_unexpected_error:` diag with the exc type surfaced.** A revert to the narrow except fails loud. **No new lesson minted** (OP-22 anti-bloat — the bug is C7, already a documented theme; the fix+guard IS the encoding, matching the 03:36 fire).
> - **VALIDATED (paste-real, $0 pure-Python):** new 7 + existing RTH-gate 6 = **13 green**; module re-imports clean post-rewire; pre-commit safety gate PASS (29 tests + 5 curated suites). Commit **57cef40** (scoped add — only my 2 files; working tree has unrelated mods per L164).
> - **NEXT FIRE picks up:** `WATCHER-FEED-REARM-CONFIRM` is now stronger — on the next RTH, BOTH the ET-gate (3e8ed79) and the load-fallback (57cef40) fixes are in; read `watcher-live-diag.jsonl` + `watcher-observations.jsonl` and confirm full 09:30–15:55 ET coverage → re-arm `watcher_feed critical=True`. If darkness STILL recurs, the diag will now show WHICH path failed (`load_data_unexpected_error:*`, `no_bars_after_topup`, `yfinance_topup_failed:*`) instead of nothing — or, if still truly zero rows, the cause is upstream of `main()` (task not firing / import crash / machine state). ALL author inboxes EMPTY. Standing direction holds (premium axis exhaustively dead L182/L183/L184; sizing-overlay closed #9): COMPOUND live edge #1 `vwap_continuation` (recency-RED → base size; license_monitor pings J on RED→green) + passive GEX forward-bank. Loop-closing fallback: `CLAUDE-INDEX-FOLD-BATCH` (24 unindexed, needs interactive/lesson-author).

## [2026-06-21] DTE × STOP-CONSTRUCTION MATRIX (the STOP-DENOMINATOR lever the diagonal disproof named) — VERDICT: **CLEAN_1DTE_UPGRADE. The campaign's FIRST clean shippable improvement to the LIVE money-maker.** A DOLLAR-ANCHORED stop turns WP-8's 1DTE SHARPE_TRADEOFF into a clean win: OOS exp/tr +$36→+$74 (2.03×) AND maxDD IMPROVES −$939→−$880 AND Sortino 14.3→25.7 AND 6/6 posQ AND structural+L173 PASS. The prior entry below called the structure/expiry frontier EXHAUSTED and named the one untested lever: "the STOP DENOMINATOR (percent-stop scaled to the long-leg premium, or a chart/level-only stop)." That lever was tested as a full DTE × stop matrix — and it WORKED. The 1DTE upgrade on the live `vwap_continuation` edge is now a CLEAN WIN, not a J dollars-vs-Sharpe tradeoff.** SAFE research (Sunday, $0, READ-ONLY — no detector/params/risk_gate/orchestrator/heartbeat/simulator_real edit, no orders). Sim `backtest/autoresearch/_dte_stop_construction.py` (validated; reuses `_dte_expansion_sim` settlement byte-for-byte + the LIVE `_edgehunt_vwap_continuation` detector byte-for-byte). ALL numbers independently confirmed against the raw JSON (C7).
> - **THE LEVER WAS REAL.** Every prior DTE test used the live −8% PERCENT stop. WP-8's maxDD-doubling (−$939 0DTE → −$1,944 1DTE) was ENTIRELY an artifact of applying a fixed PERCENT to the bigger 1DTE premium (median entry premium grows $2.55→$3.57→$4.48 with DTE → a fixed −8% = a bigger DOLLAR loss). **The unexplored dimension was the stop CONSTRUCTION, not the structure.** Tested 4 constructions × {0,1,2}DTE at the live ITM-2 tier, 166 signals, real OPRA fills, 2025-01-02..2026-06-16.
> - **WINNER = b_dollar_anchored @ 1DTE (the ONLY clean-win cell in the 12).** Cap the per-trade DOLLAR loss at **$67.68** (= median per-trade loss on the 85 0DTE −8% losers at ITM-2, calibrated once at 0DTE then applied UNCHANGED at 1DTE) instead of capping the percent: **OOS exp/tr +$36.34 → +$73.91 (+$37.57, 2.03×); OOS total $1,817 → $3,770; maxDD −$939.12 → −$879.84 (BETTER than the 0DTE baseline by $59, not worse); Sortino 14.31 → 25.70 (+80%); worst day −$223.68 → −$67.68 (well inside Safe −$600 / Bold −$835); positive quarters 5/6 → 6/6; structural+L173 PASS.** WR barely moves (42.8% → 41.6%) — the dollar cap trims only the fat-tail stop-outs, NOT the body of winners → it did NOT repeat the diagonal's lift-collapse failure mode.
> - **WHY it works (the core finding):** with the −8% PERCENT stop, maxDD scales with the premium (−$939 → −$1,944 → −$3,008 across DTE) because a fixed percent of a bigger premium is a bigger dollar loss. The dollar-anchored cap holds the dollar loss flat at $67.68 regardless of DTE → 1DTE maxDD collapses BELOW the 0DTE baseline while the gentler-theta dollar-lift survives. **Capping the dollar loss, not the percent, is the lever.**
> - **What did NOT transfer (honesty, C3/L172):** the CHART/LEVEL stop posts a seductive 70.5% WR at 1DTE but is a theta/tail trap — FAILS L173 oos_drop_top5 at EVERY DTE, OOS total collapses to $587 at 1DTE, maxDD −$3,480. Hypothesis "chart-stop becomes viable at 1DTE" REJECTED. The PERCENT-SCALED stop (per-DTE pct holding the median dollar-loss equal to 0DTE: {0:−8%, 1:−5.71%, 2:−4.55%}) is only a PARTIAL — Sortino 18.87 but maxDD +48% > the +25% bar (it caps the MEDIAN dollars, not the right tail). **No 2DTE cell clean-wins under ANY stop** (two overnight sessions reintroduce a gap/settlement tail the per-trade dollar cap can't reach; worst day −$1,140 blows the Bold kill). Switched book strictly below directional-always on Sharpe/Sortino/total at every NEUTRAL policy (directional-always $7,065.96 / Sharpe 3.883 / Sortino 5.753 vs best switched $6,323.12 / 3.693 / 5.021). **Sweep: 0/108 cells pass all bars, 0/108 where condor beats directional on its own chop days; best-thesis cell still −$134.40.** Bars across all NEUTRAL policies: rr_up=FALSE, rec_dd_down=TRUE (trivially — by routing away from the bleeding sleeve while sacrificing more upside), no_regress=FALSE, oos_pos=TRUE.

## [2026-06-21] DEBIT-SPREAD-ify PIVOT — refine the LIVE edges into defined-risk debit verticals to fix the recency drawdown; VERDICT: DEAD / thesis FALSIFIED (the spread can only ADD downside vs the tight −8%-stopped baseline)

> **Signal J wakes to (OP-25). The most direct attempt yet at the LIVE problem (the recency drawdown) — and an honest FALSIFICATION.** Diagnosed mechanism: the −8% premium stop whipsaws LONG single-leg calls in the chop regime (9-11/11 recent losers exited EXIT_ALL_PREMIUM_STOP). Thesis: a DEBIT vertical (long near + SHORT a further-OTM strike, same direction) caps premium-at-risk + cuts theta/vega/whipsaw WITHOUT touching the entry signal → risk-adjusted rescue even if capped upside lowers raw EV. **Resolves DIRECTION-BACKLOG #2.**
> - **Built TDD-first, real OPRA fills, $0:** `backtest/lib/simulator_debit.py` (debit = inverse of credit; reuses `simulator_credit.py`'s shared OPRA loader byte-for-byte) — **15/15 unit tests** (realized bounded in [−debit, width−debit] · no-look-ahead entry strictly on the bar AFTER decision · expiry-intrinsic settler exact for call+put · credit-priced geometry SKIPs); **credit suite still 17/17 = no regression** from the shared-loader reuse. Detectors reused byte-for-byte; NO edits to watchers/params/risk_gate/orchestrator/heartbeat.
> - **VERDICT: all 3 target edges = NO.** **#1 `vwap_continuation` (the LIVE edge IN the drawdown, PRIMARY target) = NO_IMPROVEMENT — ZERO of 54 cells improve BOTH book maxDD AND recency-total while OOS-positive.** The auto-picked "best" cell (long ATM / short +$4 OTM) shows higher full exp ($96.14 vs $48.33) + a huge recent total (+$2,232 vs +$408, JSON-verified) **only because `stop_frac=0.99` REMOVES the −8% stop** (exit_hist = 93 PT / **3 STOP** / 20 EOD of 116) + width=$4 lets winners run — "no tight stop + ride wide," NOT the spread capping risk. Its book maxDD = **−$829.8 vs the long baseline −$454.56 (WORSE by $375)**, Sortino **0.31 vs 0.88 (WORSE)** → FAILS L175 on BOTH maxDD + Sortino. **#2 = RISK_REDUCED_LOWER_EV** (maxDD shallower but Sharpe 0.45→0.07 + Sortino 1.06→0.09 collapse + EVERY cell flips OOS-NEGATIVE). **#4 = NO_IMPROVEMENT** (recency bleed AMPLIFIED, all 9 cells deeper maxDD).
> - **MECHANISM (C3, honest root cause):** the live single-leg's shallow maxDD (−$454/−$612) IS a direct consequence of the tight −8% stop (caps each loss ~$39/3-lot). A debit vertical's defined-risk floor (~$326/3-lot) only binds BELOW the −8% stop's floor → against a TIGHTLY-stopped baseline the spread can only ADD downside. The one point in the spread's favor is BUYING-POWER (net debit ~$109/lot vs single-leg premium up to ~$491/3-lot) — a sizing argument, NOT drawdown reduction; on a $-for-$ equity-curve basis the spread is strictly worse here. **The live edge is ALREADY survivable in chop via its tight stop** (recent degraded but NET POSITIVE +$408/+$666, not RED).
> - **No RESCUE_IMPROVEMENT cleared → no live-path/WP work appended. Do NOT ship; keep the long single-leg with the −8% stop.** The lever for recency-rescue is the ENTRY/regime filter or stop calibration (C28), NOT a debit spread. Scorecard: `analysis/recommendations/PIVOT-SPREADIFY-SCORECARD.md`. Harnesses: `backtest/autoresearch/_spreadify_vwap_continuation.py` + `_spreadify_vwap_reclaim.py`; results `analysis/recommendations/spreadify-{vwap_continuation,vwap_reclaim}.json`.
> - **HIGHEST-EV NEXT SELF-DIRECTED PIVOT: DIRECTION-BACKLOG #3 — regime-adaptive STRUCTURE switch.** Both the long-directional book (drawing down in chop) AND the premium-selling pivot point the same way: the missing piece is CLASS-selection by regime, not a per-trade gate (those die by winner-removal, L174) and not the option STRUCTURE on a single edge (just FALSIFIED). Build a PORTFOLIO that SELLS the (mechanical, null-validated) condor sleeve on causally-classified low-vol/range days and BUYS the directional debit/long sleeve on trend days, gated on a causal regime read (VIX level+slope, overnight range/ATR, trend-strength), validated as a BOOK (beats either class alone, no-regression). Why #3 over #4b (wide-OPRA-fetch) / DTE-expansion: #3 is testable on data we already hold (both sleeves' detectors + sims exist), directly attacks the chop-regime drawdown that #2 and the deploy-timing batch just failed to fix by other means, and is the natural child of two consecutive walls (per-trade gating dead + spread-structure dead → the only untried axis is class-switching). #4b and DTE both need a heavy OPRA data-fetch first → queue behind #3.

## [2026-06-21] PREMIUM-SELLING PIVOT — NEW strategy CLASS built + tested (theta KILLS long premium → SELL it); DEAD as a ratifiable edge, IC = LEAD pending a wide-OPRA-band fetch

> **Signal J wakes to (OP-25). The single most important attempted find of the campaign — and an honest WALL.** Self-directed off the C3/L58 structural insight: ~64 long-premium families died because BUYING 0DTE premium pays theta + fights delta. SELLING defined-risk premium INVERTS the theta sign — and should profit in the range/chop regime currently drawing down our bull-directional book. First-ever test of a *selling* CLASS (market-neutral, defined-risk, in-scope 0DTE SPY options).
> - **Built TDD-first, real fills, $0:** `backtest/lib/simulator_credit.py` + `multileg_structures.py` ALONGSIDE the untouched `simulator_real.py` (reuses its OPRA loader + fill conventions byte-for-byte); `test_simulator_credit.py` **17/17 PASS** (both-shorts-OTM nets +credit · breached PCS never worse than max_loss · no-look-ahead entry strictly on the bar AFTER decision · real per-leg cache fills, not synthesized). 900-cell grid (IC/PCS/CCS/IB/BWIC × 180), 365d real OPRA multi-leg fills.
> - **VERDICT: DEAD as a ratifiable edge; IC retained as a LEAD.** **CCS (assigned bear-income): DEAD** (0/150 OOS-positive, best −$4.27/tr, inside the random null). **Iron Fly: DEAD** (full −$2.1/tr, −$1,378 book DD — the steamroller's favorite; "tightest range = highest credit" is exactly its blast radius). **PCS: DEAD** (−$10 to −$14/tr). **IC (neutral OTM condor): LEAD-not-EDGE — clears 7 of 8 gates** (OOS **+$22.95/tr**, 85% WR, posQ 5/6 monthly, drop-worst5 +$21, IS-2025-H1 +$28.96, recency-chop +$8.84) **but FAILS gate 6 — the L172 random-strike null: randomizing the short offset {2,3,4} reproduces & at p95 EXCEEDS (+$26.03) the chosen offset → generic theta, NOT strike selection. Two independent harness passes + two null seedings = same verdict.** Per OP-11/L172 this is the named exception to the standing profitable-edge authorization → **NOT shippable.**
> - **Regime-diversification thesis = PARTIALLY confirmed:** the condor IS positive in the recent chop (the regime hurting the bull book) but does NOT amplify there (+$8.84 < +$23 OOS) AND that chop edge is itself null-positive (generic theta). Premium-selling SURVIVES chop; it does not hand us a *selected* diversifying edge on this data.
> - **Root cause (why the steamroller wins here):** the ±$5 OPRA cache band forces near-ATM (0.6%-OTM) short legs with 6:1 risk:credit narrow $1-2 wings — it CANNOT price the textbook 16-delta / 20-30-wide condor. The benign tail is conditional on the narrow wings (max-loss $100-200/lot); it does NOT generalize to a real ~$3,000-max-loss condor. **Inverse-direction confirmation of C3/L58/L172: selling inverts the theta SIGN but not the L172 test.**
> - **Highest-EV next structural pivot (the only LEAD→EDGE path):** fetch a WIDE, delta-targeted OPRA band (±$15-20 → true 16-delta short + 20-30-wide wing) → re-test the REAL max-loss tail inside the kill-switch → find a SELECTION rule (VIX-character / realized-range / time-regime) that BEATS the random-strike null. Filed DORMANT as **WP-PS1** (`markdown/planning/LIVE-PATH-WORKPACKAGE.md`); CLASS added to the backlog TALLY (`markdown/research/STRATEGY-HUNT-BACKLOG.md`). Scorecard: `analysis/recommendations/PIVOT-PREMIUM-SELLING-SCORECARD.md`.

**TWO real bugs caught by firing it live in-session (vs. trusting the nightly fire — this is exactly why):**
1. **Conductor budget $1.50 → $10** (`run-conductor.ps1`). First fire aborted at t+1s: "Exceeded USD budget (1.5)" doing ZERO work. `--max-budget-usd` counts cumulative input tokens + sub-agent rollup, so an opus+high-effort fire loading CLAUDE.md + the gamma agent's full MCP surface blows $1.50 instantly (same class as run-heartbeat.ps1:163). The Q&A path called bare `["claude",...]`; under the pythonw/Task-Scheduler minimal PATH that raised FileNotFoundError ("[claude CLI not found on PATH]" landed in J's Discord). Fixed to resolve the absolute `claude.exe` (mirrors `_shared.ps1` $ClaudeExe); verified launches rc=0 v2.1.183. ship/shelve bus is pure-Python and was already working.

**PROOF — the conductor's first real fire (exit 0, ~6 min, fully autonomous):** the HARVESTER-FALSE-CRIT-GUARD entry directly below. It found a real problem, shipped a tested guard, pruned 24 phantom criticals, filed a lesson, and respected every rail — with no human in the loop.

## [2026-06-18] CONTEXT-108: DECISION-LEDGER corruption fixed at the PRODUCER level (WRITE side)

The state-contract test surfaced `decisions.jsonl` (56/122 rows corrupt) and `aggressive/decisions.jsonl` (168/427 corrupt). Root cause = multiple competing producers writing INCOMPATIBLE formats into the same append-only file: pretty-printed multi-line `json.dump(...indent=2)` (the `'Field required'` fragments in the safe ledger), concatenated objects on one physical line with no trailing `\n` (the `'Extra data'`/`'Expecting property name'` rows in the aggressive ledger), and schema drift (`bear_score` vs `bearish_score`, `action` vs `decision`, missing `tick_id`/`date`, literal-string `"position_status":"null"`). Existing rows are IMMUTABLE (untouched); this fixes the WRITERS so every NEW row is clean. Complements the earlier kitchen_daemon `errors='replace'` READ-side fix.

**Canonical writer shipped:** `backtest/lib/ledger.py#append_decision(path, row)` — validates the row against `DecisionRowModel`, serializes compact single-line (`json.dumps(..., separators=(",",":"))`) + `"\n"`, appends utf-8 + flush. One row → one line → always valid, or it raises `StateContractError` and writes NOTHING (fail-closed; a malformed row never reaches disk). Test: `backtest/tests/test_ledger_writer.py` (4 passed) proves clean-write + fail-loud-on-invalid + no-partial-append-on-existing-ledger.

**Canonical schema PINNED:** `action` (NOT `decision`), `bull_score`/`bear_score` (NOT `bearish_score`), required `tick_id`+`date`+`action`. Chosen because the MAJORITY of consumers already index these names: both heartbeat.md templates, `eod_deep/main.py` (CONTEXT-106 fixed it FROM `d["decision"]` TO `d["action"]` — confirming the pin), `pattern_backtest.py`, `near_miss_audit.py`, `shadow_model_eval.py`. = `DecisionRowModel` as it already stood; the writer just enforces it at the boundary.

**Python producer repointed:** `setup/scripts/backfill_decisions.py` (the only Python writer of the canonical `decisions.jsonl`) now calls `append_decision` instead of its ad-hoc `open('a')`+`json.dumps`. Imports verified, py_compile clean, state-contract test still 16/16 green (findings unchanged — no existing data mutated).

**Satellite ledgers intentionally NOT forced through `DecisionRowModel`** (they are DIFFERENT artifacts, not the corrupted canonical ledger, and are already single-line + newline-terminated): `fast-path-decisions.jsonl` (`fast_path_executor.py` — its rows use `decision`/`account`/`decided_at_utc`/`placed`; it reads its OWN ledger back expecting `rec.get("decision")` at L408, so forcing `action`/`tick_id`/`date` would break it) and `shadow-model-decisions.jsonl` (`shadow_model_eval.py` — comparison-scorecard rows with `real_action`/`shadow_action`, not a decision row). Forcing either through the decision contract would REJECT their valid rows — a correctness regression, not a fix.

> **NOTE FOR THE HEARTBEAT-OWNING WAVE (heartbeat.md + aggressive/heartbeat.md — NOT edited here):** the heartbeat PROMPT is the PRIMARY writer of the canonical `decisions.jsonl` (the LLM emits the JSON line literally per the prose template), and is therefore the remaining source of NEW multi-line / concatenated / key-reordered / `"null"`-string drift. When that wave touches the prompt, pin the emitted row to the canonical schema: **single compact line, no trailing-object concatenation, keys `tick_id`(int) + `date`(str) + `action`(str) REQUIRED, scores as `bull_score`/`bear_score`, `position_status` a real JSON `null` (not the string `"null"`)**. Match exactly the shape `append_decision` validates (`DecisionRowModel`). Ideally route the prompt's write through a tiny Python shim that calls `append_decision` so the LLM never hand-serializes JSON. Until then, the writer + contract guard the Python producers; the prompt remains the one un-guarded writer.

- stray 0-byte `backtest/crypto/__init__.py` shadows the real `crypto` pkg (pattern_backtest self-heals; delete the file for the clean root fix); cross-account circuit-breaker schemas divergent (C9); `news.json` staleness reads wrong key (cosmetic); dashboard watcher panel deferred (color-map fix shipped).
- **Audit caveat:** even the audit agents had partial visibility — the gate-audit agent cited a stale scorecard and didn't read CONTEXT-105's WF conclusions. Cross-checked here.
